 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Item&nbsp;2.02 Results of Operations and Financial Condition. </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Item&nbsp;7.01 Regulation FD Disclosure. </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The information in this report furnished pursuant to Items 2.02 and 7.01, including Exhibits 99.1 and 99.2 attached hereto, shall not be deemed
&#147;filed&#148; for the purposes of Section&nbsp;18 of the Securities and Exchange Act of 1934, as amended (the &#147;Exchange Act&#148;), or otherwise subject to the liabilities of that section. It may only be incorporated by reference in another
filing under the Exchange Act or the Securities Act of 1933, as amended, if such subsequent filing specifically references the information furnished pursuant to Items&nbsp;2.02 and 7.01 of this report. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">On January&nbsp;20, 2015, Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. (the &#147;Company&#148;) announced its financial position and results of operations as
of and for its fiscal quarter and year ended December&nbsp;27, 2014 in an earnings press release that is attached hereto as Exhibit 99.1. Attached hereto as Exhibit 99.2 is financial information and commentary by Devinder Kumar, Senior Vice
President and Chief Financial Officer of the Company, regarding the Company&#146;s fiscal year ended December&nbsp;27, 2014 and fiscal quarter ended December&nbsp;27, 2014. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To supplement the Company&#146;s financial results presented on a U.S. Generally Accepted Accounting Principles (&#147;GAAP&#148;) basis, the
Company&#146;s earnings press release and CFO commentary contain non-GAAP financial measures, including non-GAAP gross margin, non-GAAP operating expenses, non-GAAP operating income, non-GAAP net income (loss), non-GAAP earnings (loss) per share,
adjusted EBITDA and non-GAAP free cash flow. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Specifically, these non-GAAP financial measures reflect adjustments based on the following:
</P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Goodwill impairment</U>: represents impairment charges related to the analysis the Company performed during its annual goodwill
impairment testing. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Restructuring and other special charges, net</U>: represents gains and losses associated with sale and leaseback
activities, employee severance costs, asset impairments, facility charges and contract termination charges associated with our restructuring plans and executive officer separation charges. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Lower of cost or market inventory adjustment</U>: represents write-down of the Company&#146;s inventory to its lower of cost or market
value. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Amortization of acquired intangible assets</U>: represents amortization expenses of acquired identifiable intangible assets in
connection with the Company&#146;s acquisitions of ATI Technologies Inc. and SeaMicro, Inc. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Workforce rebalancing severance
charges</U>: represents employee severance costs associated with a reduction of the Company&#146;s global workforce as part of the ongoing transformation and diversification strategy. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Loss on debt redemption</U>: represents losses that the Company incurred as a result of the repurchase of certain outstanding indebtedness
in excess of the carrying amount of the debt. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><U>Legal settlements, net</U>: represents various licenses and settlements regarding
patent-related matters. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Item&nbsp;9.01 Financial Statements and Exhibits. </B></P>
<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">

Reports 2014 Fourth Quarter and Annual Results </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>SUNNYVALE, Calif. &#151; January&nbsp;20, 2015 &#151; </B><U>AMD</U> (NASDAQ:AMD)
today announced revenue for the fourth quarter of 2014 of $1.24 billion, operating loss of $330 million and net loss of $364 million, or $0.47 per share. Non-GAAP<SUP STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">(1)</SUP> operating income was $36
million, non-GAAP<SUP STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">(1)</SUP> net income of $2 million and breakeven non-GAAP<SUP STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">(1)</SUP> earnings per share. </P>

</TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">&#147;We made progress diversifying our business, ramping design wins and improving our balance sheet this
past year despite challenges in our PC business,&#148; said Dr.&nbsp;Lisa Su, AMD president and CEO. &#147;Annual Enterprise, Embedded and Semi-Custom segment revenue increased over 50% as customer demand for products powered by our high-performance
compute and rich visualization solutions was strong. We continue to address channel headwinds in the Computing and Graphics segment and are taking steps to return it to a healthy trajectory beginning in the second quarter of 2015.&#148; </P>

<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">All Other category operating loss was $383 million compared with $28 million in Q3 2014 and operating income of $21 million in Q4 2013. The sequential and year-over-year decreases are primarily due to a $233 million
goodwill impairment charge, $71 million restructuring and other special charges, net and a $58 million lower of cost or market inventory adjustment. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Recent Highlights </B></P> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Strong demand continued for AMD-based game consoles, with Microsoft and Sony having shipped nearly 30&nbsp;million consoles to-date. Sony also recently announced plans to <U>release PlayStation<SUP
STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">&reg;</SUP>4 in the People&#146;s Republic of China</U>. </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">AMD <U>added &#147;Carrizo&#148; and &#147;Carrizo-L&#148; to its 2015 Mobile APU roadmap</U>, giving customers a single platform that scales from high-performance notebook gaming PCs to mainstream laptops. With new
energy efficiency features, next generation CPU cores and the latest GCN graphics, &#147;Carrizo&#148; will be AMD&#146;s most advanced APU ever when it comes to market in the first half of 2015. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>

<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">AMD drove continued adoption of high-performance APUs into new embedded markets with key new customer design wins, including: </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">

<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>

range of Linux and Windows based development projects. </TD></TR></TABLE>

<U>M78</U> desktops &#150; by companies worldwide, including China Mobile Communications Corporation, Dr.&nbsp;Pepper Snapple Group and Telcel (Mexico). </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>

<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">AMD launched its <U>Catalyst Omega driver suite</U>, delivering performance increases of up to 19 percent on AMD Radeon&#153; graphics<SUP STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">(2)</SUP>&nbsp;and up to 29 percent on
AMD APUs,<SUP STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">(3) </SUP>more than 20 new features, like Virtual Super Resolution, and improvements based on user feedback, as well as a set of new developer tools and Linux optimizations. Downloads to date
total 8.8 million. </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>

<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Demonstrating thought leadership in gaming and graphics, AMD introduced <U>AMD FreeSync technology</U>,<SUP STYLE="font-size:85%; vertical-align:top">(4)</SUP> an innovative, open-standards-based screen synching
technology that maximizes the reduction of input latency and reduces or fully eliminates stuttering and tearing during gaming and video playback on select AMD Radeon&#153; graphics cards and current and future generation APUs. FreeSync-enabled
displays from BenQ, LG Electronics, Nixeus, Samsung and Viewsonic were showcased at the 2015 International Consumer Electronics Show and are expected to be available in market starting in the first quarter of 2015. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>

for extreme-scale computing in conjunction with projects associated with the U.S. Department of Energy (DOE). The </P></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Current Outlook </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">AMD&#146;s outlook
statements are based on current expectations. The following statements are forward-looking, and actual results could differ materially depending on market conditions and the factors set forth under &#147;Cautionary Statement&#148; below. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">For Q1 2015, AMD expects revenue to decrease 15 percent, plus or minus 3 percent, sequentially. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">For additional details regarding AMD&#146;s results and outlook please see the CFO commentary posted at <U>quarterlyearnings.amd.com</U>. </P>

conference call for the financial community at 2:30 p.m. PT (5:30 p.m. ET) today to discuss its fourth quarter and annual financial results. AMD will provide a real-time audio broadcast of the teleconference on the <U>Investor Relations</U> page of

<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">AMD (NASDAQ:AMD) designs and integrates technology that powers millions of intelligent devices, including personal computers, tablets, game
consoles and cloud servers that define the new era of surround computing. AMD solutions enable people everywhere to realize the full potential of their favorite devices and applications to push the boundaries of what is possible. For more
information, visit <U>www.amd.com</U>. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><I>Cautionary Statement </I></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; text-indent:4%; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">This earnings press release and the conference call remarks contain forward-looking statements concerning AMD; its expected first quarter of
2015 revenue; the trajectory of the Computing and Graphics segment; and the features, functionality and availability of its future products, which are made pursuant to the safe harbor provisions of the Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of
1995. Forward-looking statements are commonly identified by words such as &#147;believes,&#148; &#147;expects,&#148; &#147;may,&#148; &#147;will,&#148; &#147;should,&#148; &#147;seeks,&#148; &#147;intends,&#148; &#147;pro forma,&#148;
&#147;estimates,&#148; &#147;anticipates,&#148; &#147;plans,&#148; &#147;projects,&#148; &#147;would&#148; and other terms with similar meaning. Investors are cautioned that the forward-looking statements in this release are based on current
beliefs, assumptions and expectations, speak only as of the date of this release and involve risks and uncertainties that could cause actual results to differ materially from current expectations. Risks include the possibility that Intel
Corporation&#146;s pricing, marketing and rebating programs, product bundling, standard setting, new product introductions or other activities may negatively impact AMD&#146;s plans; that AMD will require additional funding and may be unable to
raise sufficient capital on favorable terms, or at all; that customers stop buying AMD&#146;s products or materially reduce their operations or demand for AMD&#146;s products; that AMD may be unable to develop, launch and ramp new products and
technologies in the volumes that are required by the market at mature yields on a timely basis; that AMD&#146;s third-party foundry suppliers will be unable to transition AMD&#146;s products to advanced manufacturing process technologies in a timely
and effective way or to manufacture AMD&#146;s products on a timely basis in sufficient quantities and using competitive process technologies; that AMD will be unable to obtain sufficient </P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">
manufacturing capacity or components to meet demand for its products or will not fully utilize its projected manufacturing capacity needs at GLOBALFOUNDRIES, Inc. (GF) microprocessor
manufacturing facilities; that AMD&#146;s requirements for wafers will be less than the fixed number of wafers that it agreed to purchase from GF or GF encounters problems that significantly reduce the number of functional die it receives from each
wafer; that AMD is unable to successfully implement its long-term business strategy; that the completion and impact of the 2014 restructuring plan and AMD&#146;s transformation initiatives could adversely affect AMD; that AMD inaccurately estimates
the quantity or type of products that its customers will want in the future or will ultimately end up purchasing, resulting in excess or obsolete inventory; that AMD is unable to manage the risks related to the use of its third-party distributors
and add-in-board (AIB) partners or offer the appropriate incentives to focus them on the sale of AMD&#146;s products; that AMD may be unable to maintain the level of investment in research and development that is required to remain competitive; that
there may be unexpected variations in market growth and demand for AMD&#146;s products and technologies in light of the product mix that it may have available at any particular time; that global business and economic conditions will not improve or
will worsen; that PC market conditions will not improve or will worsen; that demand for computers will be lower than currently expected; and the effect of political or economic instability, domestically or internationally, on AMD&#146;s sales or
supply chain. Investors are urged to review in detail the risks and uncertainties in AMD&#146;s Securities and Exchange Commission filings, including but not limited to the Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q for the quarter ended September&nbsp;27, 2014.

<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">1.</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">In this earnings press release, in addition to GAAP financial results, AMD has provided non-GAAP financial measures including non-GAAP operating income, non-GAAP net income (loss), non-GAAP earnings (loss) per share and
non-GAAP gross margin. These non-GAAP financial measures reflect certain adjustments as presented in the tables in this earnings press release.&nbsp;AMD also provided&nbsp;adjusted EBITDA and non-GAAP free cash flow as supplemental measures of its
performance. These items are defined in the footnotes to the selected corporate data tables provided at the end of this earnings press release.&nbsp;AMD is providing these financial measures because it believes this non-GAAP presentation makes it
easier for investors to compare its operating results for current and historical periods and also because AMD believes it assists investors in comparing AMD&#146;s performance across reporting periods on a consistent basis by excluding items that it
does not believe are indicative of its core operating performance and for the other reasons described in the footnotes to the selected data tables. Refer to the data tables at the end of this earnings press release. </TD></TR></TABLE>

<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">2.</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Intel Core i7 4690X with 16GB DDR3-1866, AMD Radeon&#153; R9 290X Windows 8.1 64-bit comparing launch drivers 13.12 and 14.501. All tests run at 3840x2160. Bioshock Infinite @ ultra scored 30.47 vs 36.24.
</TD></TR></TABLE>

<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">3.</TD>

DOF=normal DISTORTION=off LENSFLARES=off LIGHTSHAFTS=off REFLECTIONS=off AO=normal we see an uplift from 34.96FPS to 45.2FPS. </TD></TR></TABLE>

<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">4.</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">FreeSync is an AMD technology designed to reduce or eliminate screen tears in games and videos by allowing the monitor&#146;s refresh rate to be controlled by and synchronized to the Radeon R-series graphics card or APU
Radeon R-series graphics.&nbsp;Requires DisplayPort 1.2a compliant monitors that support DisplayPort Adaptive-Sync and an AMD desktop 2014 A-series APU with Radeon R-series graphics, with forthcoming FreeSync-enabled driver.&nbsp;Support for use
with multiple monitors planned. Confirm supported technologies with system manufacturer before purchase. </TD></TR></TABLE>

<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">*</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">The Company presents adjusted EBITDA as a supplemental measure of its performance. Adjusted EBITDA for the Company is determined by adjusting operating income (loss) for depreciation and amortization, employee
stock-based compensation expense and amortization of acquired intangible assets. In addition, the Company also included the following adjustments for the indicated periods: for fourth quarter of 2014, the Company included an adjustment for goodwill
impairment, net restructuring and other special charges and lower of cost or market inventory adjustment; for 2014, the Company included an adjustment for goodwill impairment, net restructuring and other special charges, lower of cost or market
inventory adjustment and workforce rebalancing severance charges; for the fourth quarter of 2013, the Company included an adjustment for net legal settlements; and for 2013, the Company included adjustments for net restructuring and other special
charges and net legal settlements. The Company calculates and communicates adjusted EBITDA in the earnings press release because the Company&#146;s management believes it is of importance to investors and lenders in relation to its overall capital
structure and its ability to borrow additional funds. In addition, the Company presents adjusted EBITDA because it believes this measure assists investors in comparing its performance across reporting periods on a consistent basis by excluding items
that the Company does not believe are indicative of its core operating performance. The Company&#146;s calculation of adjusted EBITDA may or may not be consistent with the calculation of this measure by other companies in the same industry.
Investors should not view adjusted EBITDA as an alternative to the GAAP operating measure of operating income (loss) or GAAP liquidity measures of cash flows from operating, investing and financing activities. In addition, adjusted EBITDA does not
take into account changes in certain assets and liabilities as well as interest and income taxes that can affect cash flows. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">**</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">The Company also presents non-GAAP free cash flow in the earnings press release as a supplemental measure of its performance. Non-GAAP free cash flow is determined by adjusting GAAP net cash provided by (used in)
operating activities for capital expenditures. The Company calculates and communicates non-GAAP free cash flow in the financial earnings press release because the Company&#146;s management believes it is of importance to investors to understand the
nature of these cash flows. The Company&#146;s calculation of non-GAAP free cash flow may or may not be consistent with the calculation of this measure by other companies in the same industry. Investors should not view non-GAAP free cash flow as an
alternative to GAAP liquidity measures of cash flows from operating activities. The Company has provided reconciliations within the earnings press release of these non-GAAP financial measures to the most directly comparable GAAP financial measures.
</TD></TR></TABLE>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="right"><B>Exhibit 99.2 </B></P> <P STYLE="font-size:12pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt" ALIGN="right">


<IMG SRC="g853202img11.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
 </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>AMD Reports Annual and Fourth Quarter 2014 Results &#150; CFO Commentary </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman" ALIGN="center"><B>January&nbsp;20, 2015 </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><I>Reconciliation
for all non-GAAP financial measures discussed in this commentary to the most directly comparable GAAP financial measures is included below and in our financial tables that accompany our earnings press release available at quarterlyearnings.amd.com.
</I></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>2014 Annual Results Highlights </U></B></P> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">AMD revenue of $5.51 billion, up 4% year-over-year. </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Gross margin of 33%, down 4 percentage points year-over-year and non-GAAP gross margin of 34%, down 3 percentage points year-over-year. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Operating loss of $155 million and non-GAAP operating income of $235 million, compared to GAAP and non-GAAP operating income of $103 million in 2013. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Net loss of $403 million, loss per share of $0.53 and non-GAAP net income of $51 million, non-GAAP earnings per share of $0.06, compared to a GAAP and non-GAAP net loss of $83 million, loss per share of $0.11 in 2013.
</TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>2014 Year End Summary </U></B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">In 2014,
AMD improved non-GAAP financial performance, while continuing to transform for long-term growth. The company achieved year-over-year revenue growth, while making progress on its revenue diversification goals, with approximately 40% of revenues
coming from high-growth adjacent markets in 2014. Non-GAAP net income profitability improved to $51 million from a loss of $83 million in 2013, despite continued weakness in the Computing and Graphics business. With a continued focus on operating
expense discipline, non-GAAP operating expenses were $1.66 billion, down 11% from the prior year. AMD&#146;s balance sheet remained strong, ending the year with over $1 billion of cash, cash equivalents and marketable securities and successfully
re-profiling the majority of term debt maturities out to 2019 and beyond. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" WIDTH="100%" BORDER="0" STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" ALIGN="center">

<TR>
<TD WIDTH="47%"></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom"></TD>
<TD WIDTH="5%"></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom" WIDTH="1%"></TD>
<TD WIDTH="46%"></TD></TR>
<TR STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt">
<TD VALIGN="top"><B>AMD 2014 and Q4-14 CFO Commentary</B></TD>
<TD VALIGN="bottom"><FONT STYLE="font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>

<TD VALIGN="bottom"><FONT STYLE="font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">


 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt" ALIGN="right">


<IMG SRC="g853202img11.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
 </P> <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>2014 Commentary </U></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Revenue</B> was $5.51 billion, up 4% sequentially. Computing and Graphics segment revenue was down 16% from 2013, primarily due to lower chipset and desktop
processor sales. Enterprise, Embedded and Semi-Custom segment revenue was up 51% from 2013, primarily due to higher semi-custom SoC sales. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP
gross margin</B> was 34% in 2014, down 3 percentage points from 2013, due to increased semi-custom SoC sales and decreased client revenue. To derive non-GAAP gross margin for 2014, we excluded the impact of the $58 million of lower of cost or market
inventory adjustment. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Operating expenses</B> in 2014 were $1.99 billion and <B>non-GAAP operating expenses</B> were $1.66 billion or 30% of revenue.
</P> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><B>R&amp;D</B> was $1.07 billion, 19% of revenue. </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="5%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><B>SG&amp;A</B> was $604 million, 11% of revenue. </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To derive non-GAAP operating expenses for 2014, we excluded
the impact of a goodwill impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges, amortization of acquired intangible assets and workforce rebalancing severance charges. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP operating income</B> was $235 million. <B> </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To
derive non-GAAP operating income for 2014, we excluded the impact of a goodwill impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges, lower of cost or market inventory adjustment, amortization of acquired intangible assets and workforce
rebalancing severance charges. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP net income</B> was $51 million. <B> </B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To derive non-GAAP net income for 2014, we excluded the impact of a goodwill impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges, loss on debt
redemption, lower of cost or market inventory adjustment, amortization of acquired intangible assets and workforce rebalancing severance charges. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>

<TD WIDTH="14%">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="2%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left">&#149;</TD>
<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top">Approximately $10 million of these charges related to our former CEO&#146;s departure, of which $5 million is cash. </TD></TR></TABLE>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>Q4 2014 Commentary </U></B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Revenue</B> was $1.24
billion, down 13% sequentially. Computing and Graphics segment revenue was down 15% from Q3 2014, primarily due to lower desktop processor and GPU sales. Enterprise, Embedded and Semi-Custom segment revenue was down 11% sequentially, primarily due
to seasonally lower semi-custom SoC sales. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP gross margin</B> was 34%, down 1 percentage point sequentially. Q3 2014 gross margin of 35%
included a $27 million, or a 2 percentage point benefit from revenue related to technology licensing. To derive non-GAAP gross margin for the quarter, we excluded the impact of the $58 million of lower of cost or market inventory adjustment. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Operating expenses</B> were $690 million and <B>non-GAAP operating expenses</B> were $382 million or 31% of revenue. </P>
<P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">

<TD WIDTH="1%" VALIGN="top">&nbsp;</TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><B>R&amp;D</B> was $238 million, 19% of revenue. </TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6pt;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE; font-family:Times New Roman; font-size:10pt" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">

excluded the impact of a goodwill impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges and amortization of acquired intangible assets. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Operating

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">


 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt" ALIGN="right">


<IMG SRC="g853202img11.jpg" ALT="LOGO">
 </P> <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP operating income</B> was $36 million. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To derive non-GAAP operating income for Q4 2014, we excluded the impact of a goodwill impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges, lower of
cost or market inventory adjustment and amortization of acquired intangible assets. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP net income</B> was $2 million. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To derive non-GAAP net income for Q4 2014, we excluded the impact of a goodwill impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges, lower of cost or
market inventory adjustment and amortization of acquired intangible assets. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Depreciation and amortization, excluding amortization of acquired
intangible assets</B>, was $44 million, compared to $46 million in the prior quarter. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Interest expense</B> was $41 million, down $2 million from the
prior quarter, largely due to debt repurchases of approximately $75 million in the quarter. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Tax benefit</B> was $3 million in the quarter, up from a
tax provision of $2 million in the prior quarter due to a tax credit. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP net income per share</B> was breakeven, calculated using
781&nbsp;million diluted shares. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Adjusted EBITDA</B> was $96 million, down $37 million from the prior quarter. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>Q4 2014 Segment Results </U></B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Computing and Graphics
segment revenue</B> was $662 million, down 15% sequentially, primarily due to lower desktop processor and GPU sales. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Computing and Graphics operating loss</B> was $56 million, compared to an operating loss of $17 million in Q3 2014, primarily due to lower channel sales
partially offset by lower operating expenses. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Enterprise, Embedded and Semi-Custom segment revenue</B> was $577 million, down 11% compared to the
prior quarter, driven primarily by decreased sales of our semi-custom SoCs. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Enterprise, Embedded and Semi-Custom operating income</B> was $109
million, essentially flat from operating income of $108 million in the prior quarter, primarily due to seasonally lower semi-custom product sales offset by higher royalties. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>GLOBALFOUNDRIES (GF) Wafer Supply Agreement (WSA) </U></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Our total purchases from GF in 2014 were approximately $1 billion, lower than the previously estimated $1.2 billion, due to lower fourth quarter purchases. The
fourth amendment to the WSA is complete with no associated penalties or special payments. We are currently negotiating our 2015 WSA amendment, in line with our business expectations.</P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>Balance Sheet </U></B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Cash, cash equivalents and
marketable securities</B> were $1.04 billion at the end of Q4 2014, compared to $938 million in the prior quarter. </P>
 <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>

due 2020 and 7.50% Senior Notes due 2022 in the open market, paying approximately 96 cents on the dollar. These repurchases were funded with our secured revolving line of credit which carries a significantly lower interest rate than these notes.

 </P> <p STYLE="margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:0pt ; font-size:8pt">&nbsp;</P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP free cash flow</B> was $94 million, with <B>net cash provided by operating activities</B> of $116
million and <B>capital expenditures</B> of $22 million. Capital expenditures decreased $7 million from Q3 2014, and free cash flow was up $105 million from Q3 2014. </P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">Beginning in Q1 2015, in line with many of our technology peers and to better reflect ongoing operations, presentation of our <B>non-GAAP results </B>will no
longer include the impact of employee stock-based compensation. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B><U>Outlook </U></B></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The following statements concerning AMD are forward-looking and actual results could differ materially from current expectations. Investors are urged to review
in detail the risks and uncertainties in AMD&#146;s Securities and Exchange Commission filings, including but not limited to the Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q for the quarter ended September&nbsp;27, 2014. </P>

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman"><B>Non-GAAP Measures </B></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">To supplement the financial results of
Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. (AMD or the Company) presented on a U.S. GAAP (GAAP) basis, this commentary contains non-GAAP financial measures, including non-GAAP gross margin, non-GAAP operating expenses, non-GAAP operating income, non-GAAP net
income (loss), non-GAAP earnings (loss) per share, Adjusted EBITDA and non-GAAP free cash flow. These non-GAAP financial measures reflect certain adjustments, and the Company has presented a reconciliation of GAAP to non-GAAP financial measures in
the tables below. The Company is providing these financial measures because it believes this non-GAAP presentation makes it easier for investors to compare its operating results for current and historical periods and also because the Company
believes it assists investors in comparing the Company&#146;s performance across reporting periods on a consistent basis by excluding items that it does not believe are indicative of its core operating performance and for the other reasons described
in the footnotes to the selected data tables contained in the earnings press release. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Company presented &#147;adjusted EBITDA&#148; in this
commentary as a supplemental measure of its performance. Adjusted EBITDA for the Company is determined by adjusting operating income (loss) for depreciation and amortization, employee stock-based compensation expense and amortization of acquired
intangible assets. In addition, the Company also included the following adjustments for the indicated periods: for the fourth quarter of 2014, the Company included an adjustment for a goodwill </P>

impairment charge, restructuring and other special charges and lower of cost or market inventory adjustment; for 2014, the Company included an adjustment for a goodwill impairment charge,
restructuring and other special charges and lower of cost or market inventory adjustment and workforce rebalancing severance charges; and for 2013, the Company included an adjustment for net restructuring and other special charges and net legal
settlements.&nbsp;The Company calculates and communicates adjusted EBITDA because the Company&#146;s management believes it is of importance to investors and lenders in relation to its overall capital structure and its ability to borrow additional
funds. In addition, the Company presents adjusted EBITDA because it believes this measure assists investors in comparing its performance across reporting periods on a consistent basis by excluding items that the Company does not believe are
indicative of its core operating performance. The Company&#146;s calculation of adjusted EBITDA may or may not be consistent with the calculation of this measure by other companies in the same industry. Investors should not view adjusted EBITDA as
an alternative to the GAAP operating measure of operating income (loss) or GAAP liquidity measures of cash flows from operating, investing and financing activities. In addition, adjusted EBITDA does not take into account changes in certain assets
and liabilities as well as interest and income taxes that can affect cash flows. </P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">The Company also presents non-GAAP free cash flow in this commentary as
a supplemental measure of its performance. Non-GAAP free cash flow for the Company was determined by adjusting GAAP net cash provided by (used in) operating activities less capital expenditures. The Company calculates and communicates non-GAAP free
cash flow because the Company&#146;s management believes it is important to investors to understand the nature of this cash flow. The Company&#146;s calculation of non-GAAP free cash flow may or may not be consistent with the calculation of this
measure by other companies in the same industry. Investors should not view non-GAAP free cash flow as an alternative to GAAP liquidity measures of cash flows from operating activities. </P>

<P STYLE="margin-top:12pt; margin-bottom:0pt; font-size:10pt; font-family:Times New Roman">This commentary contains forward-looking statements concerning AMD; its financial outlook for the first quarter of 2015 and fiscal 2015, including revenue,
gross margin, non-GAAP operating expenses, employee stock-based compensation, the total of interest expense, taxes and other expense, inventory, taxes and capital expenditures; its ability to be non-GAAP profitable in 2015; its optimal cash, cash
equivalents and marketable securities balances; and restructuring charges and expected cash payments related to any restructuring charges, which are made pursuant to the Safe Harbor provisions of the Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of 1995.
Forward-looking statements are commonly identified by words such as &#147;would,&#148; &#147;may,&#148; &#147;expects,&#148; &#147;believes,&#148; &#147;plans,&#148; &#147;intends,&#148; &#147;projects&#148; and other terms with similar meaning.
Investors are cautioned that the forward-looking statements in this document are based on current beliefs, assumptions and expectations, speak only as of the date of this document and involve risks and uncertainties that could cause actual results
to differ materially from current expectations. Risks include that Intel Corporation&#146;s pricing, marketing and rebating programs, product bundling, standard setting, new product introductions or other activities may negatively impact AMD&#146;s
plans; that AMD will require additional funding and may be unable to raise sufficient capital on favorable terms, or at all; that customers stop buying AMD&#146;s products or materially reduce their operations or demand for AMD&#146;s products; that
AMD may be unable to develop, launch and ramp new products and technologies in the volumes that are required by the market at mature yields on a timely basis; that AMD&#146;s third-party foundry suppliers will be unable to transition AMD&#146;s
products to advanced manufacturing process technologies in a timely and effective way or to manufacture AMD&#146;s products on a timely basis in sufficient quantities and using competitive process technologies; that AMD will be unable to obtain
sufficient manufacturing capacity or components to meet demand for its products or will not fully utilize its projected manufacturing capacity needs at GLOBALFOUNDRIES, Inc. (GF) microprocessor manufacturing facilities; that AMD&#146;s requirements
for wafers will be less than the fixed number of wafers that it agreed to purchase from GF or GF encounters problems that significantly reduce the number of functional die it receives from each wafer; that AMD is unable to successfully implement its
long-term business strategy; that the completion and impact of the 2014 restructuring plan and AMD&#146;s transformation initiatives could adversely affect AMD; that AMD inaccurately estimates the quantity or type of products that its customers will
want in the future or will ultimately end up purchasing, resulting in excess or obsolete inventory; that AMD is unable to manage the risks related to the use of its third-party distributors and add-in-board (AIB) partners or offer the appropriate
incentives to focus them on the sale of AMD&#146;s products; that AMD may be unable to maintain the level of investment in research and development that is required to remain competitive; that there may be unexpected variations in market growth and
demand for AMD&#146;s products and technologies in light of the product mix that it may have </P>
